# Grandfathered prose naming a retired `supports` grade (see
# scripts/check_retired_support_prose.py and issue #12805).
# Each line is `count<TAB>path<TAB>sentence`, where count is how many
# times that sentence names a retired value in that file. A mention
# fails the guard if it is absent here OR appears MORE often than the
# count recorded. This file only ever shrinks: fix the prose, then run
#   just update-retired-support-prose-baseline
